2026-11-20T04:35:22.0544044Z The goal is a proposition, so `have` is preferred over `haveI`.
2026-11-20T04:35:22.0544673Z The difference between `have` and `haveI` is that `haveI` inlines the value.
2026-11-20T04:35:22.0545142Z But this is not relevant for proofs because of proof irrelevance.
2026-11-20T04:35:22.0545371Z 
2026-11-20T04:35:22.0545538Z Note: This linter can be disabled with `set_option linter.style.haveILetI false`
